### [1] [Scalable Unitary State-Encoding for Generic Large Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09760)
*Vittorio Pagni,Peter K. Schuhmacher,Michael Felderer,Michael Epping,Kevin Lively*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出确定性的可扩展振幅编码方法，利用张量网络将全控多路复用器电路化简为单/双比特门，在低双比特门代价下实现深度与保真度可调权衡，并在IQM硬件上验证了优异保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将数据高效编码进量子态是量子计算协议的基本瓶颈；默认态制备方法电路深度过大，使应用在首次计算前就被噪声淹没，限制了近期硬件的实用性。

Method: 提出两种互补算法：其一将冗余门控制的消除形式化为布尔逻辑最小化问题；其二借助单个辅助比特，利用门角度的Walsh-Hadamard谱将多控旋转门映射为单/双比特操作序列。两种方法无需振幅放大或中电路测量，并可用张量网络编码超出经典内存容量的任意复数数据或可查询函数；两者可混合使用。

Result: 在全连接图上系统表征了电路总深度随系统规模与目标保真度的变化；混合方法与Qiskit默认态制备相比，在相同保真度下电路深度提升最多达数个数量级；在具有方格子连接性的IQM硬件上演示，即使仅用最少的噪声缓解也获得优异保真度。

Conclusion: 大幅降低编码门深使原本在第一步计算前即被噪声淹没的应用可在当前及近期硬件上实验执行，缩短了实现稳健量子实用性的时间尺度。

Abstract: Efficient encoding of data into quantum states remains a fundamental bottleneck in many quantum computing protocols, which would otherwise offer advantages over their classical counterparts. Here, we introduce deterministic and scalable amplitude-encoding methods with low two-qubit gate costs and tunable trade-offs between circuit depth and encoding fidelity. Our approaches require no amplitude amplification or mid-circuit measurement and are capable of encoding arbitrary complex data or queryable functions by using tensor-networks to go beyond what can be held in classical memory. Our principle contributions are two complementary algorithms for simplifying fully controlled multiplexer circuits to one- and two-qubit gates. In the first, we formally map the elimination of redundant gate controls to a Boolean logic minimization problem, while in the second we utilize a single ancilla qubit to map the multi-controlled rotation gates to a sequence of one- and two-qubit operations via the Walsh-Hadamard spectrum of the gate angles. We benchmark these methods across a broad range of function classes, characterizing their total depth in an all-to-all connectivity graph against system size and target fidelity. Hybridizing our methods gives up to orders of magnitude improvement in circuit depth at equivalent state fidelity compared to the default state-preparation method in the Qiskit package. We furthermore demonstrate our method on IQM hardware with a square lattice connectivity, finding excellent fidelity, even with minimal noise mitigation. By significantly reducing gate depth for encoding, this work enables the experimental execution of applications on current and near-term hardware which would otherwise be reduced to noise before the first calculation step, shortening the time horizon to robust quantum utility.

</details>


### [2] [Integral zeros of Krawtchouk polynomials and quantum entanglements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08518)
*Jaeseong Heo,Young-Hoon Kiem,Chul-hee Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文解决了二元Krawtchouk多项式整数零点问题中三类非对称参数族的完整分类，并指出剩余情形为有限个零星零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heo与Kiem(2019)将二元Krawtchouk多项式整数零点问题与量子信息中满足range criterion的子空间对存在性相联系，但仅解决了对称情形k=ℓ，非对称情形悬而未决。

Method: 针对三类无限参数族(m,n,k,ℓ)分别进行系统分析，结合数论与组合方法判定其整数零点存在性。

Result: 完全求解了三类非对称参数族：(3,t²−1,t(t−1)/2,t(t+1)/2)、(4,3t²+2t−1,(3t²−t)/2,(3t²+5t+2)/2)与(4,3s²+4s,(3s²+s)/2,(3s²+7s+4)/2)。

Conclusion: 非对称情形除已解决的三类参数族外，其余仅为零星零点；对每个m≥5仅有有限多个。

Abstract: In Heo and Kiem (2019), the authors raised a problem relating integral zeros of binary Krawtchouk polynomials, parametrized by quadruples $(m,n,k,\ell)$ with $m\le n$ and $k\le \ell$, to the existence of pairs of subspaces satisfying the range criterion in quantum information theory, and settled the symmetric case $k=\ell$. In this paper, we solve the problem for the following three asymmetric families:
  $(m,n,k,\ell)=(3,t^2-1,t(t-1)/2,t(t+1)/2)$, $t\ge2$;
  $(m,n,k,\ell)=(4,3t^2+2t-1,(3t^2-t)/2,(3t^2+5t+2)/2)$, $t\ge2$;
  $(m,n,k,\ell)=(4,3s^2+4s,(3s^2+s)/2,(3s^2+7s+4)/2)$, $s\ge1$.
  The remaining cases are sporadic zeros, with only finitely many for each $m\ge5$.

</details>


### [3] [Thermodynamics of Speed and Efficiency with Quantum Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08564)
*Yue Liu,Ryotaro Honma,Ruicheng Bao,Tan Van Vu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为连续监测的跳跃反馈开放量子动力学推导出有限时间电流—耗散关系，并由此建立量子速度极限与稳态信息引擎的功率—效率权衡，定量刻画反馈通过信息熵与直接动力学两条途径对量子态变换和引擎性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速热力学操作通常伴随不可逆耗散，导致速度与热力学成本之间存在权衡；而量子反馈如何从根本上重塑这一权衡尚不清楚，缺乏定量基础。

Method: 针对带跳跃反馈的连续监测开放量子动力学，推导有限时间电流—耗散关系；由该基本关系导出量子速度极限及稳态信息引擎的功率—效率权衡，并将反馈效应分解为信息熵贡献与直接动力学贡献。

Result: 得到反馈影响态变换与引擎性能的显式分解；在有限时间信息擦除中实现广义Landauer界，并在集体引擎的大系统极限下于有限功率处逼近单位信息效率。

Conclusion: 该工作为存在反馈控制的快速、低耗散量子热力学提供了定量基础，拓展了速度—耗散权衡在反馈情形下的理论框架。

Abstract: Rapid thermodynamic operations generally entail irreversible dissipation, giving rise to trade-offs between operating speed and thermodynamic cost. Yet, how quantum feedback fundamentally reshapes these trade-offs remains elusive. Here, we derive a finite-time current--dissipation relation for continuously monitored open quantum dynamics with jump feedback. From this fundamental relation, we establish a quantum speed limit and a power-efficiency trade-off for stationary information engines, identifying how feedback affects state transformations and the performance of engines through both informational entropy and direct dynamical contributions. Our results provide a quantitative foundation for fast, low-dissipation quantum thermodynamics in the presence of feedback control, exemplified by attaining the generalized Landauer bound in finite-time information erasure and approaching unit information efficiency at finite power in the large-system limit of a collective engine.

</details>


### [4] [Magic Is in the Eye of the Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08586)
*Zhi-Heng Shen,Zhewei Yin,Hao-Lin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文比较了两量子比特体系中四种magic度量：稳定子Rényi熵（SRE）、magic的最小/最大相对熵（min-/max-relative entropy）以及对数自由鲁棒性（logarithmic free robustness），在精确证明下确定各自的最大magic态，并指出不同度量导致极值magic物理结论（如弱混合角）依赖度量选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: magic（非稳定化性）是通用量子计算的量子资源，但其度量不唯一。高能与核物理应用几乎只用SRE，缺少对其他度量行为的系统比较，因此需要考察极值magic结论是否依赖度量。

Method: 在两量子比特空间中，将SRE与三种基于稳定子态优化的度量（magic的最小相对熵、最大相对熵、对数自由鲁棒性）进行比较；对四种度量给出最大magic态的精确解析证明；并在Møller散射中由最小magic确定弱混合角的框架下，比较各度量给出的sin²θ_W取值。

Result: 四种度量的最大magic态各不相同，即极值态取决于度量。由最小magic确定弱混合角时，最小相对熵给出随能量变化的值，与SRE不同；而最大相对熵与对数自由鲁棒性在所有能量下均给出sin²θ_W = 1/4。该差异源于基于优化的度量具有非解析结构。

Conclusion: 由极值化magic得到的物理结论可能依赖所选的magic度量；在高能与核物理中仅依赖SRE的做法需谨慎，应关注度量非唯一性带来的可观测差异。

Abstract: Magic, or nonstabilizerness, is the quantum resource behind universal quantum computation. Its applications in high-energy and nuclear physics rely almost exclusively on the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE), although magic has no unique measure. For two qubits, we compare the SRE with three measures defined through optimizations over stabilizer states: the min- and max-relative entropies of magic and the logarithmic free robustness. We determine the maximal-magic states of all four measures, with exact proofs, and find that they depend on the measure. In the determination of the weak mixing angle from minimal magic in Møller scattering, the min-relative entropy gives an energy-dependent value that differs from that of the SRE, whereas the max-relative entropy and the logarithmic free robustness give $\sin^2θ_W=1/4$ at all energies. We trace this difference to the non-analytic structure of the optimization-based measures. Physical conclusions drawn from extremizing magic can thus depend on the measure.

</details>


### [5] [Universality in Quantum Many-Body Spin Ratchets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08590)
*Andrea Azzali,Marko Ljubotina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明量子多体自旋棘轮的普适漂移速度在所有时刻严格成立，仅需平移不变性与U(1)对称性，并推广到平均平移不变的随机电路情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前后续工作仅证明可积、SU(2)对称量子自旋棘轮的漂移速度具有普适性，并对非可积情形提出猜想；作者希望给出严格的普遍性证明并明确其成立所需的最弱条件。

Method: 研究作用于偶数/奇数格点为不同自旋类型的交替自旋链上的量子多体自旋棘轮电路，该设置破坏宇称对称性并呈现手性输运；通过解析证明在仅要求平移不变性与U(1)对称性的条件下漂移速度的普适形式在所有时刻精确成立，并进一步在随机电路中证明平均平移不变性已足够。

Result: 普适漂移速度对所有时刻严格成立，无需可积性与SU(2)对称性；平均平移不变的随机电路亦给出相同结果；一旦破坏平移不变性，漂移速度立即偏离该普适预言。

Conclusion: 量子自旋棘轮的普适漂移由平移不变性与U(1)对称性共同决定，是精确且稳健的性质；平移不变性是必要要素，其破坏会导致可观测的偏离。

Abstract: We consider a class of quantum circuits, known as quantum many-body spin ratchets, which act on alternating spin chains with two distinct spin types on even and odd sites. This setup breaks parity symmetry and exhibits chiral transport. It has been shown that the drift velocity in the case of integrable, SU(2)-symmetric quantum spin ratchets is universal, and conjectured that the same holds in the non-integrable case. Here, we prove that this universal drift is exact at all times, requiring only translation invariance and U(1) symmetry. Furthermore, we prove that it is sufficient for the circuit to be translation-invariant on average by deriving the same result in the random case. Finally, we demonstrate that breaking translation invariance immediately results in deviations from this universal prediction.

</details>


### [6] [Exploring topological signatures of quantum Fisher information scaling in graph states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08593)
*Majid Hassani,Patrick Emonts,Jordi Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文数值研究图的 clique complex 的 Betti 数等拓扑不变量能否刻画图态量子 Fisher 信息（QFI）是否超越标准量子极限（SQL），发现拓扑优化可导向具有计量优势的图态，但 Betti 数本身并不决定 QFI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图态是量子计量的重要资源，其 QFI 有基于图的闭式表达（统计具有相同邻域的顶点对数目），但哪些全局结构性质决定 QFI 是否超过 SQL 尚不清楚，作者因此检验 Betti 数这一拓扑不变量是否可作为判据。

Method: 采用两种互补的数值方法：(1) 在平衡完全多部图上随机加边或删边——该类图同时具有超 SQL 的 QFI 与单个非零高阶 Betti 数；(2) 从 QFI 通常不超过 SQL 的 Erdős–Rényi 随机图出发，用 Monte Carlo 搜索最大化选定的 Betti 数。

Result: 加边会同时破坏计量优势与非平凡拓扑；删边时高阶 Betti 数在 QFI 已降至 SQL 后仍长期存在。以 Betti 数为目标的 Monte Carlo 搜索将随机图推向近极端的多部结构，其 QFI 显著超过 SQL。

Conclusion: 拓扑优化是获得计量有用图态的一条途径，但 Betti 数单独并不决定 QFI，二者并非一一对应的判据关系。

Abstract: Graph states are a versatile resource for quantum metrology, and their quantum Fisher information (QFI) has a closed-form expression in terms of the underlying graph: it counts pairs of vertices with identical neighborhoods. It remains unclear, however, which global structural properties of a graph determine whether its QFI exceeds the standard quantum limit (SQL). Here we ask whether topological invariants, namely the Betti numbers of the graph's clique complex, capture this behavior, and we address the question numerically with two complementary approaches. First, we randomly add or delete edges in balanced complete multipartite graphs, which combine a QFI beyond the SQL with a single nonvanishing higher Betti number. Edge addition eventually destroys both the metrological advantage and the nontrivial topology. Under edge deletion, by contrast, higher Betti numbers persist well after the QFI has dropped to the SQL. Second, we start from Erdős--Rényi random graphs, whose QFI does not typically exceed the SQL, and maximize a chosen Betti number with a Monte Carlo search. The search drives the graphs toward near-extremal multipartite structures whose QFI lies well beyond the SQL. Our results show that topological optimization offers a route to metrologically useful graph states, but also that Betti numbers alone do not determine the QFI.

</details>


### [7] [Harnessing dimensional leakage for continuous-variable randomness certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08607)
*Moisés Alves,Tailan S. Sarubi,Vitor L. Sena,Alexandre B. Tacla,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文表明，在几乎量子比特（almost-qudit）框架下，对全部输入集合取平均可显著缓解高维泄漏对随机性认证的危害，从而用低压缩单模高斯态加零差探测实现实验可及的连续变量半器件无关随机性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半器件无关协议通常依赖严格的希尔伯特空间维度假设，而连续变量光学场本质上是无限维的，实际实现中难以满足；几乎量子比特框架虽能量化目标子空间外的泄漏，但在固定制备与测量输入下，泄漏会严重破坏随机性认证的可靠性。

Method: 在几乎量子比特框架下分析泄漏对随机性认证的影响，将分析由固定的制备—测量输入对扩展为对整个输入集合求平均，并据此评估低压缩单模高斯态配合零差探测的认证能力。

Result: 对完整输入集合求平均可减轻泄漏带来的脆弱性，并扩大可容忍的泄漏范围；在此增强鲁棒性下，低压缩单模高斯态与零差探测也能获得非零的随机性认证量。

Conclusion: 平均化处理提升了几乎量子比特方案对维度泄漏的鲁棒性，给出了一条基于高斯态与零差探测的实验友好型连续变量半器件无关随机性认证路径。

Abstract: Semi-device-independent quantum protocols often rely on strict Hilbert-space dimension assumptions that are difficult to satisfy in realistic implementations, particularly with infinite-dimensional optical fields. The almost-qudit framework addresses this issue by quantifying the leakage of quantum states beyond a target subspace. Although such leakage can severely compromise randomness certification for fixed preparation and measurement inputs, we show that averaging over the full input ensemble can mitigate this vulnerability and extend the range of tolerable leakage. This enhanced robustness enables nonzero randomness certification using low-squeezing single-mode Gaussian states and homodyne detection, thus offering an experimentally accessible continuous-variable implementation.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum-enabled measurement of gravitational forces at the milligram scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08619)
*Matthew Herbst,Ewa Rej,Joe Depellette,Richa Cutting,Jinhoon Jeong,Mika A. Sillanpää*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在毫开尔文温度下，利用87 mg源质量的微米级相干位移对加载1.3 mg测试质量的振动薄膜产生引力印记，并采用微波光力学方案测量薄膜振动，实现了小尺度引力传感两个数量级的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力是已知相互作用中最弱的，在微观尺度上小质量间的引力相互作用因太弱而难以实验探测；广义相对论与量子力学尚未令人满意地统一，而探测微观尺度引力（量子力学与引力可能交汇之处）的技术正逐渐成熟。开展真正的量子引力探索需要引力质量具备非经典性质，这要求极低温和小运动范围等苛刻的扰动防护条件。

Method: 在毫开尔文温度环境中，使87 mg源质量做微米级相干位移，其对加载1.3 mg测试质量的振荡薄膜运动产生引力作用；采用微波光力学（optomechanical）方案测量薄膜振动，该方案是研究机械振子非经典运动态的关键装置。

Result: 综合考虑源质量与测试质量的总质量，该装置在小尺度引力传感方面取得两个数量级的进步，并在kHz及以上频率处实现35 dB的引力灵敏度提升。

Conclusion: 该装置为研究处于非经典运动态的质量之间的引力相互作用提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: Gravity is by far the weakest among all known interactions. While it dominates the large-scale structure of the universe, gravitational interactions between small masses in the microscopic range remain underexplored because they are too weak to be experimentally accessible. The interface between gravity, described by general relativity, and quantum mechanics, which describes the microscopic world, is of particular interest. From a theory standpoint, these two major descriptions of nature have not been combined in a satisfactory manner. Technology is gradually becoming mature enough such that probing gravity at the microscopic scale, where quantum mechanics and gravity potentially meet, is becoming accessible. Explorations towards genuine quantum gravity have to be carried out under conditions where the gravitating masses possess non-classical properties, which necessitates ultimate protection against disturbances, such as ultralow temperatures and a small range of motion. Here, we present an experiment at millikelvin temperatures, where the gravity due to a micron-range coherent displacement of an 87 mg source mass imprints on the motion of an oscillating membrane loaded with a 1.3 mg test mass. Vibrations of the membrane are measured in the microwave optomechanical scheme, which is a key setup for investigating non-classical motional states of mechanical oscillators. When considering the combination of total source and test masses, our setup presents two orders of magnitude progress in sensing gravity at the small scale, as well as a 35 dB improvement of gravitational sensitivity at kHz frequencies or above. Our setup offers a plausible approach for studying gravitational interactions between masses prepared in non-classical motional states.

</details>


### [9] [What are local complementations, really?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08620)
*Kenneth Goodenough*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文将图上局部补运算重新表述为稳定子态（拟阵）基的切换操作，从而揭示图论描述难以直接得到的结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部补运算表面上是很奇特的图操作，但因刻画图态之间的局部Clifford等价而成为图态研究中的基本概念。作者希望找到更自然、更本质的表述框架，以看清局部补运算背后的结构。

Method: 沿用Bouchet的思路，把图上的局部补运算解释为稳定子态基（拟阵基）的切换（switching），并以此为基础重新推导相关性质；同时以该框架作为多拟阵（multimatroid）在量子信息中应用的入门介绍。

Result: 基与切换的视角揭示了稳定子态的一些结构性质，如顶点子式（vertex-minor）以及局部Pauli测量的统计特性，这些性质仅从图论描述中不易看出；此外该框架还简化了若干关于“图在局部补运算下等价”的既有证明。

Conclusion: 局部补运算本质上是拟阵基的切换，多拟阵为研究稳定子态与图态提供了更基础、更富结构性的语言，并可作为量子信息中多拟阵应用的入门。

Abstract: A local complementation at a vertex $v$ of a graph $G$ replaces the induced neighborhood of $v$ with its complement. Local complementations are, at face value, peculiar operations. Yet they famously show up when describing the local Clifford equivalence among graph states, and, because of this, have become a fundamental notion in the study of graph states. Following Bouchet, we show here how local complementations on graphs are more naturally thought of in terms of switchings of (matroid) bases of stabilizer states. Bases and switchings reveal structural properties of stabilizer states that are harder to glean from the graph-theoretic description alone, such as vertex-minors and statistics of local Pauli measurements. Bases also simplify prior proofs about graphs up to local complementations, of which we give several examples. Bases are foundational in the study of (multi)matroids; as such this paper serves as a gentle introduction to the applications of multimatroids to quantum information theory.

</details>


### [10] [How Many Shots Does a Quantum Reservoir Need? Measurement Cost and Its Scaling in Buffered Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08632)

TL;DR: 论文区分量子储备池计算中的"精度预算"N_ε（将储备池特征估计到固定相对精度所需测量次数）与"收敛预算"N_★（使训练后读出逼近其无噪声上限所需测量次数），并指出两种定义系统性不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QRC中唯一可训练的是线性读出，但每个特征都是有限次测量下的期望值估计，测量次数（shot预算）成为无经典对应的一阶超参数。已有研究开始关注其影响，但"所需预算应如何定义"尚无定论，且不同定义是否一致未被澄清。

Method: 形式化定义精度预算N_ε与收敛预算N_★，并通过命题（Proposition 1，confound）分析N_★与无噪声误差下界之间的关系。

Result: N_★在构造上依赖于无噪声误差下界，导致误差下界更差（表现更劣）的配置反而显得"更便宜"；两种预算定义的分歧是系统性的而非偶然的。

Conclusion: 不应以各配置达到自身天花板所需预算来比较，而应在固定测量预算下比较不同配置。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing (QRC) inherits from classical reservoir computing the property that only a linear readout is trained, but unlike a classical reservoir its state is never directly observable: every feature is an expectation value estimated from a finite number of measurement shots. The shot budget is therefore a first-class hyperparameter with no classical analogue. Its effect on QRC has recently begun to be studied; what remains unsettled is how a required budget should be defined, and whether competing definitions agree. This paper argues that they do not, and that the disagreement is systematic rather than incidental.
  This paper distinguishes a \emph{precision budget} $N_\varepsilon$, the shots needed to estimate the reservoir features to a fixed relative accuracy, from a \emph{convergence budget} $N_\star$, the shots needed for the trained readout to approach its own noise-free ceiling. Proposition~\ref{prop:confound} shows that $N_\star$ depends on the noise-free error floor by construction, so that a configuration with a worse floor appears cheaper, and that configurations should therefore be compared at a fixed measurement budget rather than by the budget each needs to reach its own ceiling.

</details>


### [11] [Sign-hedged Beam QAOA:Scaling Quantum Optimization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08646)
*Anupama Ray,Arush Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出sign-hedged beam RQAOA：在标准RQAOA的基础上对变量符号保留两个分支并用束搜索（保留最优B个残差问题），再以常规RQAOA完成求解，显著缓解早期错误传播，在阻挫SK实例上把AR从0.474提升至0.998。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RQAOA通过QAOA关联符号逐个消元，但每个符号一旦确定便不可撤销，早期误判会沿后续所有约减传播并可能丢失全局最优；且随消元推进残差规模缩小而等效边密度上升，后期决策面对的正是全连接SK这类最困难区域。

Conclusion: 增益来自额外的残差电路评估、经典剪枝与小核心精确求解，而非提高p；beam是固定p下QAOA/RQAOA之上的决策侧优化，并不主张量子电路单独随比特数扩展。

Abstract: Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) encodes a combinatorial objective as an Ising Hamiltonian and alternates cost and mixer operations for p rounds. Recursive QAOA (RQAOA) scales further by using the resulting correlations, whether variables tend to agree or disagree, to eliminate variables one at a time. But each inferred sign is accepted irrevocably, so a single early mistake propagates through every later reduction and can discard the global optimum. As elimination proceeds, residuals shrink while effective edge density typically rises, so later decisions face denser Ising problems, a regime that fully connected Sherrington Kirkpatrick (SK) instances stress test directly. This paper proposes sign-hedged beam RQAOA, which retains the variable or pair selected by standard RQAOA, explores both possible sign assignments, keeps the best B residual problems according to a classical score, and completes each survivor with ordinary RQAOA. On frustrated SK instances, where standard recursion collapses to AR 0.474, beam recovers near-optimal AR 0.998 at the same p. We evaluate the workflow on 50+ instances from HamLib and QOBLIB, spanning 4 and 5 regular and Erdos Renyi MaxCut graphs, Sherrington Kirkpatrick (SK) spin glasses, and cardinality-constrained portfolio optimization, with up to 60 qubits and hardware runs on ibm kingston and ibm marrakesh. Matched simulator and device cells show consistent beam rankings: simulation and hardware agree that beam recovers near-optimal approximation ratio (AR) where standard recursion fails. These gains come from additional residual-circuit evaluations, classical pruning, and exact solution of a small core, rather than from increasing p, beam is a decision-side optimization atop fixed-p QAOA/RQAOA, not a claim that quantum circuits alone scale with qubit count.

</details>

*Marin Girard*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子码表(如Grassl表)中大量条目仅有下界而无显式码，尤其在d≥9、n≳170区域；随机码在一阶矩意义上通常已接近最优，但当表中最好已知码并不优于随机码时，存在改进空间。作者希望系统性寻找超过表下界的显式稳定子码并给出可验证的距离证书。

Conclusion: 剔除随机稳定子码可稳定超越码表下界，其优势可由简单一阶矩计数定量解释；该方法具有可扩展性，并给出了大量可验证的新码与改进条目，为后续搜索（含AI驱动搜索）提供了明确方向。

Abstract: I report 192 explicit binary stabilizer codes $[[n,k,d]]$ with $53\le n\le 254$ and $5\le d\le 13$ whose minimum distance exceeds the lower bound listed in Grassl's code tables at the same $(n,k)$: 161 codes by one and 31 by two. Through the standard propagation rules they raise 1,873 entries of the table. For 190 of the codes, a random Clifford encoder is followed by expurgation: every logical operator of weight below the target distance is enumerated, and a few logical operators are promoted to stabilizers so that each low-weight one anticommutes with at least one of them. The other two are obtained by puncturing a code from the table and repairing it in the same way. All 192 distances are exact: the lower bound is certified by exhaustive meet-in-the-middle enumeration of up to $4\times10^{13}$ Pauli operators, and the upper bound by an explicit logical operator of weight $d$. A first-moment count explains the results. Expurgated random codes have about eight more logical qubits than a typical random code of the same length and distance, so they beat the table wherever its best known codes are no better than random. The largest gains, up to nine logical qubits, are at $d\ge9$ and $n\gtrsim170$, where the table often gives a lower bound without an explicit code. The same count predicts improvements at 619 further pairs $(n,d)$. The search was run by AI agents. The codes, their certificates and an independent checker are public.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Structural descriptors provide graph neural networks (GNNs) with positional and topological inductive biases. However, widely used classical encodings based on random walks or Laplacian spectra emphasize specific structural statistics of a graph, leaving room for alternative descriptors. Neutral-atom quantum processing units (QPUs) can realize graph-dependent Rydberg Hamiltonians, allowing graph topology to be probed through dynamical correlation measurements. In this work, we use these measurements as quantum graph features (QGFs), providing a complementary, hardware-derived family of structural encoding that is not accurately described by classical features derived from Laplacian and adjacency matrices, even when supplemented with an extended set of random-walk, geometric descriptors and classical physical models. By appropriately selecting the quench parameters of the Hamiltonian, we access two characteristic correlation regimes with distinct structural signatures: antiferromagnetic-like and ferromagnetic-like. On an intrinsic information benchmark of 15 topological and spectral probing tasks, quantum and hybrid quantum-classical features match or surpass classical descriptors on local topological properties. When supplied to message-passing, attention, and graph-transformer backbones, hybrid quantum-classical features yield improvements that are within cross-fold noise on most cells but sign-consistent across architectures. When comparing noiseless, shot-sampled emulation-derived quantum features with a neutral-atom QPU implementation, the downstream hybrid results with compressed hardware features match their emulated counterparts within $0.01$ F$_1$ on all backbones. On a synthetic family of ladder graphs, QGFs distinguish two specific classes, outperforming the strongest classical structural descriptors by a margin exceeding cross-fold variability.

Abstract: Interpreting the decoherence of a spin qubit requires knowing how the surrounding bath spins behave: whether they fluctuate independently, exchange polarization incoherently, or evolve as coherent groups. These processes can yield the same Hahn-echo coherence time yet produce different coherence signals under pulse control of the qubit. Existing theoretical approaches either capture bath correlations at a cost that grows combinatorially with spin-bath cluster size or neglect polarization transport altogether. We introduce kinetic cluster embedding (KCE), which uses a single dimensionless parameter, comparing the flip-flop coupling of a spin pair with its detuning and linewidth, to decide how each pair must be treated. Pairs that can complete an appreciable exchange oscillation are propagated with the full dissipative quantum dynamics. All other spins are treated as random-telegraph fluctuators coupled through a kinetic network of polarization-transfer rates. We show that for nitrogen-vacancy centers coupled to surface electron spins, KCE propagates about 7 four-spin clusters, compared to more than 10$^5$ propagated by fourth-order master-equation cluster-correlation (ME-CCE4); nevertheless it reproduces ME-CCE4 coherence times to within 15$\%$ for dipolar couplings of order $10^{2}$ times the bath relaxation rate. For a three-dimensional P1 bath, the agreement is within 16$\%$. Coherent multispin clusters emerge as the leading correction to independent fluctuators, and they leave a distinct signature in the CPMG response. Importantly, we find that whether a rate description suffices is set not by coupling strength alone but by whether a pair can exchange polarization coherently before detuning and damping suppress it.

Abstract: We investigate how spectroscopic calibration and basis selection affect predictions for a dipolar-exchange gate between two optically trapped $^{23}$Na$^{133}$Cs molecules. Enlarging the molecular basis produces nonmonotonic changes in fidelity, although the 11-state and 25-state calculations give closely agreeing logical maps after phase alignment. TL;DR: 本文从诺特定理结合路径积分形式，将诺特流中沿经典路径消失的欧拉-拉格朗日项解释为量子涨落，并给出其泛函表达式等于负哈密顿量在偏离经典路径距离处的取值，以单谐振子为例验证了短时极限下的能量-时间不确定关系与长时极限下的零点能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诺特流推导中会出现一个对欧拉-拉格朗日方程的积分项，该项在经典路径上为零故不贡献电流；但量子粒子不必然沿经典路径运动（这正是费曼路径积分量化的基础），因此该项在量子体系中不为零，代表系统偏离经典路径所导致的诺特流量子涨落。作者希望从诺特定理与路径积分形式出发系统地计算这一涨落。

Abstract: The local distinguishability of five orthogonal product states (OPSs) has been studied in several multipartite settings, but the five-partite case still requires a systematic characterization. We prove that the OPSs corresponding to 434 of these graphs can be perfectly distinguished by local operations and classical communication (LOCC), while the remaining 9 exceptional cases fall into two types and require separate analysis. Abstract: Quantum thermodynamics seeks to characterize possible state transformations under thermodynamic constraints in microscopic quantum systems. Abstract: Perfect wave transfer (PWT) reproduces spatial reflection at a common finite time under a fixed Hamiltonian. We classify this process for open compact bosons and inhomogeneous Tomonaga--Luttinger liquids, distinguishing transfer of oscillator and neutral observables from transfer on the charged field algebra. In the single-charge model, the first two notions coincide. Global transfer additionally requires rephasing of the compact zero mode. For regular reflection-symmetric channels, exact oscillator transfer forces an odd multiple of the optical crossing time and a strictly ordered, parity-compatible spectrum differing from the conformal ladder at only finitely many levels. This spectral condition is also sufficient when the Robin boundary data induced by the physical profile are included. Requiring Neumann conditions after the Liouville transformation instead forces conformality. We construct an explicit, regular one-defect family for every $r>1/2$. Its transfer parameters are precisely the reduced fractions $r=p/q$ with odd numerator and denominator, with shortest transfer time $pL/v_0$. At the delay $3L/v_0$, the $r=3/5$ profile has numerical contrast $K_{\max}/K_{\min}\simeq 12.913$, compared with $81$ for $r=3$. Two independent conservative discretizations show second-order convergence on fixed mode bands. Occupation- and charge-constrained error estimates quantify robustness, while an exact homogeneous-chain calculation explains the $J^{2/3}$ resolved-band scale also observed in the engineered channels. These results provide a spectral design method and finite-device benchmarks for quadratic quantum channels. An interacting microscopic realization remains open.

Abstract: This paper presents a framework for integrating quantum detection techniques into a Dual-Parallel Mach-Zehnder Modulator (DPMZM)-based Next Generation Passive Optical Network 2 (NG-PON2) communication system. We present a system model for the DPMZM-based NG-PON2 quantum communication system and characterize its signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), demonstrating its superiority over conventional MZM-based systems. We analyze the performance of various quantum modulation techniques, including binary phase-shift keying, quadrature phase-shift keying, and M-ary quadrature amplitude modulation. A comprehensive analytical framework is developed to evaluate the SNR, BER, and mutual information for both classical and quantum detection schemes within the DPMZM-based NG-PON2 quantum communication system. Our simulation results show that quantum detection significantly outperforms classical detection, and the DPMZM architecture further enhances the performance over the baseline MZM architecture by improving the effective modulation efficiency and receiver sensitivity.

Abstract: We study how conservation of total spin affects the extractable work of an interacting Hubbard double quantum dot charged in the presence of unresolved quasistatic detuning fluctuations. The full fermionic Fock space is used to model preparation through electronic reservoirs, whereas the additional work deficit associated with spin conservation arises entirely from the singlet and triplet states at half filling. At zero detuning, modulation of the symmetric tunnel barrier couples two singlets of even parity. The corresponding transition matrix element vanishes when U = V . An exact formula for the additional work deficit follows from passive spectral rearrangement under two extraction constraints: conservation of particle number alone and conservation of both particle number and total spin. At low temperature, an expansion in the detuning response gives a leading estimate of the noise amplitude at which this deficit first appears. Increasing the preparation temperature changes the triplet population and produces a crossover at s2 = q, where the additional spin constraint becomes nonbinding for the averaged state, even though both ergotropies may continue to decrease. We obtain a semi-analytic expression for this crossover by separating the thermal Gibbs weights from the singlet dynamics under a fixed charging pulse. Averaging over symmetric detuning disorder also restores parity in the density matrix and allows singlet eigenvalues of different parity to exchange order, changing the branch that determines the thermal boundary. The work values are optimal over specified classes of unitary operations and do not establish the yield of a finite discharge protocol.

Abstract: Recent experimental demonstrations of Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) have attracted significant attention for their ability to establish information-theoretically secure encryption keys for next-generation communication systems. As practical deployment accelerates, the realization of compact, scalable, and high-throughput discrete-variable (DV) QKD systems has become increasingly important. While advances in photonic integration are enabling low-size, low-weight, and low-power QKD hardware, the security and performance of these systems rely heavily on efficient post-processing techniques to generate identical, secure keys. This tutorial presents a comprehensive algorithm-level review of practical post-processing techniques for DV-QKD, with particular emphasis on information reconciliation methods based on Cascade, Winnow, low-density parity-check (LDPC), and polar codes. We also analyze the theoretical asymptotic and finite-size secret key rates (SKRs) of DV-QKD and benchmark these bounds against the practical SKRs achieved using actual post-processing algorithms. Furthermore, we present a server-client model to implement the entire QKD post-processing pipeline. The performance is evaluated in terms of QBER and SKR across various system parameters, considering the complete post-processing pipeline, including sifting, information reconciliation, privacy amplification, and authentication. This tutorial also presents a graphical user interface (GUI) for the QKD post-processing methods, enabling researchers, industry practitioners, and academic users to visualize and analyze the impact of different post-processing techniques and system parameters, such as QBER, on the SKR performance of DV-QKD systems.

Abstract: Krylov subspace methods efficiently approximate extremal eigenstates and eigenvalues of many-body Hamiltonians, but non-orthogonal bases can suffer from ill-conditioning. We investigate a variational Krylov-like framework that iteratively optimizes a parametrized sequence of states to mimic Lanczos-like couplings while penalizing linear dependencies. Analyzed against exact Krylov and Lanczos on a finite Anderson impurity model, ansatz expressivity strongly influences the performance: Hamiltonian-inspired ansätze preserve Krylov-like convergence while improving the conditioning, whereas shallow ansätze remain stable but miss low-energy directions.

Abstract: How a quantum resource can be quantified depends on which operations are free. Yet two proposed quantifiers of quantum state texture can increase under free operations: the Wigner-Yanase-Dyson skew information relative to the unique textureless state and the squared Hilbert-Schmidt distance from that state. We show reachability from every state orthogonal to the textureless state, whether pure or mixed: each can be freely transformed into any other state. Therefore, every texture monotone must assign the same maximal value to all such states, a requirement that neither candidate satisfies. We then construct valid texture monotones by adding the population outside the textureless state to the skew information and by maximizing the squared Hilbert-Schmidt distance over free operations. We prove faithfulness, convexity, and invariance under textureless ancillas, characterize additivity properties and strong monotonicity, satisfied only in the Wigner-Yanase case, and derive tight bounds for both monotones at fixed overlap with the textureless state and characterize all saturating states. Together, these results show how allowed state transformations impose operational constraints on the quantification of quantum state texture.

Abstract: Time crystals are phases of matter that spontaneously break time-translation symmetry. They have been ruled out in the ground and Gibbs states of isolated systems with only short-range interactions, yet they have been proposed and observed experimentally in nonequilibrium settings such as Floquet and open systems. We first construct an explicit model and prove that coupling to an environment can activate a time crystal absent in the isolated system, even without such resource supply. We consider Lindblad master equations with (i) time-independent Hamiltonian and jump operators, (ii) time-translation covariance, and (iii) detailed balance---conditions that hold in standard derivations unless thermodynamic resources are supplied externally. Our model satisfies these conditions, and the activation occurs in a stationary state of both the isolated and open systems. We give several implementations of this dynamics with a zero-temperature Gibbs environment, a time-independent, energy-conserving interaction, and only short-range Hamiltonians, one of which exactly reproduces the time-crystalline periodicity. We then prove a no-go theorem: under (i)--(iii), activation can arise only from an environment-induced Hamiltonian term, not from dissipation. Our results show that time crystals can be activated in open systems without external resource supply and identify the physical requirements on the system and environment for this activation through our general no-go theorem.

Abstract: Free fermions exhibit Eigenstate Typicality (ET) meaning that the reduced density matrix, $ρ$, of a small spatial subsystem of a {\sl single}, typical, excited energy eigenstate is gaussian with a modular hamiltonian, $h \equiv -\log ρ$, asymptotically proportional to the free fermion hamiltonian. We revisit this argument for fermions belonging to a superposition of degenerate eigenstates. Measurements of the single particle distribution of a small subsystem would then exhibit non-thermal deviations from the FD distribution. These features may be traced to a violation of Wick's theorem for the chosen excited state. Finally, we speculate that initial conditions in a realistic fermi system evolving towards equilibrium might be encoded in the interaction terms of the modular hamiltonian and consequent correlations of fermions.

Finally, we demonstrate the utility of these tools in the context of multiscale quantum chemistry workflows using the ChemShell computational chemistry environment.

Abstract: Programmable shaping of photon-pair spectral correlations is important for frequency-bin quantum information processing. The optimized distributions achieve Pearson correlations between 0.66 and 0.98 and classical overlap fidelities between 0.32 and 0.62. Accuracy is limited by pump-phase transfer, parasitic nonlinear background, waveshaper constraints, amplitude-only filtering, and measurement noise. Joint spectral intensity contains no spectral-phase information. Therefore, the results demonstrate control of measured frequency-bin populations and correlations, not complete reconstruction of the two-photon state. Phase-sensitive characterization is required to verify inter-bin coherence and quantify the entanglement dimensionality of the output state. Our results establish direct measurement feedback as a practical approach to reconfigurable frequency-bin correlation engineering when an accurate end-to-end model is unavailable.

Abstract: Experimentally certifying polarization entanglement in annihilation photons is challenging because polarizers are unavailable in the high-energy regime and one needs to rely on Compton polarimetry. Previously proposed schemes designed for this task are either experimentally unfeasible or require a specific source state model. We address these issues by reformulating Compton scattering as a continuous-outcome quantum instrument, thereby enabling the prediction of the scattering angles and the post-scattering polarization state. Our framework allows connecting Compton polarimetry with entanglement witnesses by deriving experimentally accessible lower bounds on Bell state fidelities for arbitrary initial two-photon polarization states. We discuss how this witness can be extracted from coarse-grained coincidence measurements with detectors of almost any angular resolution. The presented scheme does not require knowledge of angle-independent empirical factors like detector efficiencies and source activity. Thus, our results provide a feasible route towards experimental entanglement certification in para-positronium decay with current technology.

Abstract: The oracle-interrogation algorithm [van Dam, FOCS '98] computes every $n$-bit Boolean function with bounded error using $n/2+O(\sqrt n)$ quantum queries. In this work, we construct an explicit family of symmetric Boolean functions requiring $n/2+Ω(n^{1/3})$ quantum queries at a fixed positive constant error. Abstract: When does a quantum particle arrive at a detector? The temporal distributions of itinerant quantum particles have been debated since the birth of quantum mechanics. Here we study the arrival-time problem in periodically monitored spin chains and quantum circuits. The arrival dynamics exhibits two regimes, the quantum Zeno and the low-monitoring regime, separated by an optimal monitoring period set by the single-particle bandwidth. In support of the theory, on superconducting quantum hardware, we measure arrival-time distributions for single and multiple interacting particles in one- and two-dimensional circuits. The measured distributions resolve both regimes and agree closely with quantum-trajectory theory. The crossover persists in interacting many-particle systems: the optimal monitoring point is preserved while many-body interference reshapes the distributions. Zeno suppression likewise persists in two-dimensional geometry. Together, these results show that the two regimes are a generic feature of monitored propagation rather than a single-particle effect. To our knowledge, our work constitutes the first experimental measurement of arrival-time distributions in a monitored quantum transport setting, addressing one of the longest-standing problems in quantum mechanics.

Abstract: The timeline by which quantum computers can solve breakthrough problems in science depends on improved quantum algorithms as much as on better hardware. Such algorithmic advances are typically achieved through many years of expert human collaboration. In mathematics and computer science, large language models have accelerated progress through automated discovery loops. Here we demonstrate automated discovery of quantum programs beyond these regimes including order-of-magnitude improvements on scientific applications. To achieve this, we build a quantum program library in the Lean theorem prover in which large language model agents synthesize novel programs against formal problem specifications and verify their correctness and cost. Agent teams collaborate, sharing results and knowledge through wikis and discussing ideas at organized conferences. Our results improve on prior baselines for parallel search (up to 1.4x), quantum arithmetic (290x), and simulations relevant to fusion, photochemistry and drug-metabolism by 11x, 8x and 2.5x respectively. Combining human and artificial intelligence at scale, under the rigor of mathematical proof, lets researchers design algorithms that today's classical and quantum computers cannot test, shortening the path to practical quantum computation.

</details>


### [63] [Deterministic Quantum Search for NP Problems Through Verifier-Based Oracle Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09830)
*Soumyajyoti Chatterjee,Harishankar Mishra,Asvija Balasubramanyam,S D Sudarsan,Gudapati Naresh Raghava*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向NP问题的广义量子oracle构造方法，并给出基于经典验证函数的确定性量子搜索框架，在保持O(√N)查询复杂度的同时实现确定性地找出满足性赋值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典穷举搜索求解NP问题最坏情况需O(N)次查询；量子搜索可将查询复杂度降至O(√N)，但常规量子搜索通常假设已有能识别解的黑箱oracle，且标准实现（如Grover）只给出概率性成功，难以直接迁移到一般NP问题。

Method: 从NP问题对应的经典验证函数出发，构造通用的量子oracle（将验证函数编译为量子线路），并设计确定性量子搜索框架，使搜索过程确定性地输出满足性赋值；同时分析构造的线路门数与量子比特资源。

Result: 所构造oracle的门数关于验证函数二进制编码长度L为多项式级，与经典验证者的多项式时间可计算性一致；总量子比特需求为O(poly(L))；搜索过程保持相对经典穷举的二次查询加速。在多个N-SAT实例上完成线路级仿真，确认可确定性地识别满足性赋值。

Conclusion: 结果表明确定性量子搜索可推广应用于一般NP问题，并在保持二次查询加速的同时克服概率性成功与oracle黑箱假设的限制，为将量子搜索用于NP求解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Problems in the class NP are important in computational science, where a given candidate solution can be verified in polynomial time, while finding a solution may require evaluating the entire search space in the worst case. Classical exhaustive approaches generally solve such problems by evaluating the verifier function over all possible inputs, resulting in a worst-case query complexity of O(N), where N = d^n represents the size of the search space, d is the number of possible values for each variable, and n is the number of variables. Quantum search algorithms can solve unstructured search problems more efficiently by reducing the query complexity to O(sqrt (N)), providing a quadratic speedup over classical exhaustive search. However, conventional quantum search formulations typically assume access to an oracle that identifies valid solutions, while standard implementations generally yield probabilistic success. In this work, we propose a generalised quantum oracle construction method for NP problems, together with a deterministic quantum search framework that identifies satisfying assignments using their corresponding classical verifier functions. The proposed framework retains the quadratic query speedup of quantum search over classical exhaustive search. We further establish that constructed oracle can be implemented using a number of gates polynomial in the size (L) of the binary encoding of verification function, consistent with the polynomial-time evaluation of the corresponding classical verifier, while the total qubit requirement remains O(poly(L)). The construction is demonstrated on multiple N-SAT instances, with circuit-level simulations confirming deterministic identification of satisfying assignments. These results establish the applicability of deterministic quantum search to NP problems while retaining the quadratic query advantage over classical exhaustive search.

</details>

*Gabriel Rabelo-Soares,Yevgeniy Kovchegov,Giorgio Torrieri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于一般量子系统的非平衡吉布斯熵定义，可基于Wigner函数计算，兼容高斯与非高斯态，并推导出相关信息论量并与Wehrl熵比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统吉布斯熵依赖平衡态系综假设，难以直接用于非平衡量子系统；同时Wigner函数虽是准概率分布，其与大数定律、中心极限定理及熵-累积量生成函数关系的联系尚需系统阐明，以便建立更普适的熵定义。

Method: 借助大偏差理论核心思想，利用重复观测累积量与Wigner函数的关系（大数定律、中心极限定理、熵与累积量生成函数的联系），基于对重复测量后底层态结构“无知”的程度，构造正定的熵定义；并推导相对熵、条件熵、互信息与Rényi熵等信息论量，针对一维谐振子对纯态与混合态与Wehrl熵进行对比。

Result: 尽管Wigner函数为准概率分布，其与可观测量的重复观测联系仍满足大数定律、中心极限定理及熵-累积量生成函数关系；据此得到正定、类吉布斯但可用于非平衡区域的熵定义，并给出若干重要性质及相对熵、条件熵、互信息、Rényi熵等推广量；在一维谐振子情形下与Wehrl熵的对比结果在摘要中未给出具体数值。

Conclusion: 该形式为非平衡量子系统提供了可行的熵框架，并讨论了从超越纠缠的量子关联到封闭量子系统热化等潜在应用。

Abstract: We propose a definition of non-equilibrium Gibbsian entropy applicable to general quantum systems, computable from Wigner functions, for both Gaussian and non-Gaussian states. We show that while the Wigner function is a quasi-probability distribution, fundamental results linking observables in repeated observations to the function, such as the law of large numbers, the central limit theorem and, crucially, the relation between the entropy and the cumulant generating function continue to apply. This ideas are the core of the large deviation theory, which allow to formulate a positive-definite definition of entropy based on our ignorance of the underlying state structure after repeated measurements of cumulants. This new definition is a parallel to the Gibbs entropy formulation, but it can also be used in out-of-equilibrium regimes. Some important properties of this entropy are discussed. In addition we derive some information theory quantities such as relative entropy, conditional entropy, mutual information and Rényi entropy. We then compare the results with Wehrl entropy for the 1D harmonic oscillator, for pure and mixed states. We conclude the the work discussing potential applications of this new formalism, ranging from quantum correlations beyond entanglement to thermalization in closed quantum systems.

</details>


### [65] [Novikov-Magnus Master Equation for Driven Quantum Systems under Classical Correlated Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09876)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化框架，将 Donsker–Furutsu–Novikov (DFN) 关系与 Magnus 展开结合，用于推导受时域相关经典噪声驱动的开放量子系统的时间局域量子主方程，获得至噪声强度四阶的 Novikov–Magnus 主方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动量子系统常受时间相关经典噪声影响，需要时间局域的量子主方程以高效描述动力学；但非对易的时序噪声扰动与噪声平均难以同时妥善处理，现有方法（如累积量展开）在精度与结构保持上存在不足。

Method: 将 DFN 关系（泛函响应闭合噪声平均）与 Magnus 展开（嵌套对易子保留噪声扰动沿受控轨迹的非对易时序作用）相结合；针对零均值高斯噪声推导至四阶的 Novikov–Magnus 主方程；并利用同一响应层级以连通高阶噪声关联加权随机映射的高阶泛函导数，推广至非高斯噪声。

Result: 在 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 噪声与带限 1/f 噪声下的驱动量子比特中，二阶与四阶映射在微扰区间内始终满足 CPTP，并准确复现随机模拟；其 diamond 范数误差与基于累积量的伪 Lindblad 主方程相当，对 OU 噪声四阶版本有小幅改进。

Conclusion: DFN–Magnus 模块化构造为驱动系统在相关经典噪声下提供了结构良好、可系统扩展（阶数与噪声统计）的时间局域主方程，保持完全正定与保迹性，精度具竞争力，并自然延拓至非高斯噪声情形。

Abstract: We introduce a modular framework for deriving time-local quantum master equations for driven systems subject to temporally correlated classical noise. The construction combines the Donsker-Furutsu-Novikov relation with the Magnus expansion: Functional response closes the noise average, while nested commutators retain the noncommuting chronological action of noise-induced perturbations along the controlled quantum trajectory. For zero-mean Gaussian noise, we derive the resulting Novikov-Magnus quantum master equation through fourth order in the noise strength. The same response hierarchy suggests a natural extension beyond Gaussian noise, with connected higher-order noise correlations weighting higher functional derivatives of the stochastic map. For a driven qubit under Ornstein-Uhlenbeck and band-limited $1/f$ noise, the second- and fourth-order maps remain CPTP throughout the perturbative regime and accurately reproduce stochastic simulations. Their diamond-norm errors are comparable to those of the cumulant-based pseudo-Lindblad master equation, with a modest fourth-order improvement for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noise.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: quintom由quintessence+phantom标量场构成，quintessence势取指数形式，仅phantom场与物质线性耦合（耦合δ）。低红移用DESI BAO+Pantheon+ SNIa(±SH0ES)，高红移用Planck CMB先验与BBN先验，MCMC统计约束H0、σ8等，并以Cassini探测器估计太阳系第五力约束。

Conclusion: 该相互作用quintom模型能给出与DESI一致的幻场穿越解，H0可在SH0ES驱动下接近局域测量，σ8偏低，且物质-幻场耦合被太阳系实验强烈限制在10⁻³量级。

Abstract: The quintom model consisting of quintessence and phantom scalar fields with linear coupling to matter is explored and constrained by updated DESI and supernovae type Ia~(SNIa) observational data. The matter interaction is assumed only between phantom and matter and the quintessence self potential is in an exponential form. The quintom evolution shows a class of solutions with phantom crossing scenario consistent with the current DESI data. From low redshift observations, we place constraints on the model with BAO data from DESI and distance measurements from SNIa Pantheon+ samples and SH0Es. For high-redshift constraints, we use CMB priors from Planck and found that the quintom model gives the best-fit Hubble parameter $H_{0}=65.780~(68.736)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ for DESI+CMB+SNIa~(+SH0ES) respectively. The marginalized statistical constrained values are $H_{0}=66.21\pm 0.84~(68.49^{+0.46}_{-0.40})$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ for DESI+CMB+SNIa~(+SH0ES) respectively. Using the BBN prior for high-redshift constraints, the corresponding best-fit values for DESI+BBN+SNIa~(+SH0ES) constraints are $H_{0}=63.999~(71.962)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ and the marginalized statistical values for DESI+BBN+SNIa~(+SH0ES) constraints are $H_{0}=61.6^{+6.6}_{-3.0}~(71.86\pm 0.88)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ respectively. Generally, adding SH0ES significantly raises the best-fit and marginalized value of $H_{0}$ for all datasets. In comparison to $Λ$CDM, the interacting quintom model gives lower values of $σ_{8}=0.74-0.79$ for combination of datasets with CMB prior. Lastly, the fifth-force constraint from local scale solar system Cassini probe is estimated, we find the constraint on the phantom-matter coupling $|δ|<3.3\times 10^{-3}$.

### [67] [Phentax: A JAX implementation of the IMRPhenomTHM waveform model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08596)
*Alessandro Santini,Diganta Bandopadhyay,Jonathan Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 我们提出 Phentax，即 IMRPhenomTHM 波形模型的 JAX 重实现，为 LISA 全球拟合算法提供快速、可批处理、可微的波形计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA 将探测数千个白矮星双星、EMRI、早期旋进的恒星级双黑洞，以及 10^4–10^7 M☉ 的大质量双黑洞等海量信号；全球拟合需对全部源参数联合推断，需评估数十万个波形模板，计算代价高昂。

Method: 选取用于模拟与分析 LISA 大质量双黑洞信号的先进时域现象学波形模型 IMRPhenomTHM（适用于可比质量双黑洞，并含高阶模式效应），用 JAX 重新实现为 Phentax。

Abstract: The upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is expected to be sensitive to thousands of white dwarf (WD) binaries in our galaxy, extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs) of stellar-mass compact objects into massive black holes, stellar-mass black hole binaries (SOBHBs) in their early inspiral phase, and, crucially, massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) with masses in the range of $10^4$-$10^7 M_\odot$. The presence of such a large number of signals requires joint inference of all parameters across all sources present in the data, an approach referred to as "global fit". This requires evaluating hundreds of thousands of waveform templates, which can be computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we present $\texttt{Phentax}$, a $\texttt{JAX}$ re-implementation of the $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$ waveform model. This is a state-of-the-art time-domain phenomenological waveform model for comparable-mass binary black holes that includes the effects of higher-order modes. Given this, $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$ is one of the models currently used to simulate and analyze MBHB signals in LISA. With $\texttt{Phentax}$, we provide a fast, easy-to-batch, and differentiable implementation of this waveform model to accelerate the development of LISA global fit algorithms.

</details>


### [68] [Effects of Self-Interactions on the Bifurcations in Boson Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08602)
*Chen Liang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究自相互作用对含一个径向节点的球对称玻色子星 ℓ=2 零模的影响，并构建由其分岔出的链状与环状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向激发的小玻色子星在静态轴对称微扰下存在零模，可由此分岔出链状和环状物质分布；但自相互作用如何影响这些零模尚不清楚。

Method: 考察两类模型：具有排斥性四次自相互作用的大质量玻色子星，以及具有Q-ball型势的孤子玻色子星；计算其 ℓ=2 零模随四次耦合与引力耦合的变化，并构造分岔出的链与环。

Result: 大质量玻色子星：在所考虑的四次耦合范围内零模始终存在；随耦合增大零模移入候选稳定分支，使该分支无法对 ℓ=2 微扰完全稳定，尽管强自相互作用可抑制径向不稳定。孤子玻色子星：分岔强烈依赖引力耦合，耦合减小时单一球对称星族可出现多个零模，链与环交替出现，低于临界耦合时零模消失；平直时空中环状Q-ball 仍存在，但未发现链，表明链的存在需要引力。

Conclusion: 自相互作用显著改变 ℓ=2 零模的存在性与位置，从而决定链状/环状分岔结构；链状构型依赖引力，而环状构型可在平直时空作为Q-ball 存在。

Abstract: Radially excited mini-boson stars have zero modes under static axisymmetric perturbations, at which they bifurcate into axisymmetric configurations with chain-like and ring-like matter distributions. We study how self-interactions affect the $\ell=2$ zero modes of spherical boson stars with one radial node, and construct the chains and rings that bifurcate from them. We consider massive and solitonic boson stars, which have a repulsive quartic self-interaction and a Q-ball type potential, respectively. For massive boson stars, the zero mode persists for all values of the quartic coupling we considered. As the coupling grows, it moves onto the candidate stable branch, so that this branch cannot be entirely stable against $\ell=2$ perturbations, even though sufficiently strong self-interactions are known to quench the radial instability of these stars. For solitonic boson stars, the bifurcations depend strongly on the gravitational coupling. As it decreases, a single family of spherical stars can have multiple zero modes, at which chains and rings alternate, and below a critical coupling the zero modes disappear. In flat spacetime, the rings still exist as static ring-like Q-balls, whereas no chains are found, which suggests that chains need gravity to exist.

</details>


### [69] [A pipeline for identifying astrophysical signals and instrumental glitches in LISA data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08880)
*Aryamann Rao,Jess McIver,Rhiannon Udall,Natalia Korsakova,Malvina Bellotti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两步流水线：先在模拟LISA数据中搜索瞬变信号，再用机器学习模型把检出事件分类为引力波或仪器毛刺（glitch），分类准确率接近98%，且笔记本电脑上分析一个月数据不到2分钟，可用于低延迟快速警报与全局拟合的初始模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA（欧空局计划2030年代发射的空间引力波探测器，工作频段0.1 mHz–0.1 Hz）将观测超大质量黑洞并合、极端质量比旋进、白矮星双星等源。与所有干涉式引力波探测器一样，LISA数据中预计含有非天体物理的瞬变（glitch）；尤其对约10^6 M_⊙量级的高质量大质量黑洞双星（MBHB），其波形形态可能与glitch相似，因此需要可靠区分。

Method: 构建两步流水线：第一步为搜索算法，在模拟LISA数据中检测潜在瞬变事件；第二步为机器学习模型，将检出瞬变分类为引力波或仪器glitch。文中并讨论了训练过程中遇到的挑战（含现有框架的局限性）。

Result: 初步结果显示分类准确率接近98%；流水线在笔记本电脑上分析一个月数据用时不足2分钟。

Conclusion: 该流水线有望支撑低延迟搜索中的准确快速警报，并可通过提供单个信号的位置与类型初步汇总来支持全局拟合分析中的模型选择；同时指出训练中的框架限制等挑战有待改进。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational wave detector scheduled for launch in the 2030s by the European Space Agency. Operating in the 0.1 mHz to 0.1 Hz band, LISA will observe a variety of astrophysical sources including but not limited to supermassive black hole mergers, extreme mass ratio inspirals, and white dwarf binaries. LISA will yield new insights, including supermassive black hole formation, new tests of general relativity, population studies of white dwarfs and others. Like all interferometric gravitational wave detectors, LISA data is expected to contain non-astrophysical transients, commonly referred to as glitches. We introduce a two step pipeline that consists of a search algorithm for detecting potential transients in simulated LISA data followed by a machine learning model that classifies the detected transients as gravitational waves or instrumental glitches. This is important for MBHBs with very high masses (around $10^6\ M_{\odot}$) which are transients that could be morphologically similar to LISA glitches in the data. Initial results demonstrate a promising ability to classify detected events with an accuracy close to $98\%$. The pipeline also shows the ability to analyze a month of data in under 2 minutes on a laptop. This shows an eventual utility of our pipeline for enabling accurate rapid alerts from low latency searches. It could also support model selection in global-fit analyses by providing an initial summary of the location and type of individual signals. This paper also discusses challenges encountered during training, including limitations of the current framework.

</details>


### [70] [Landauer bound in a regular Bardeen black hole: extremality and evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08948)
*J. Andrade,J. O. Cedeño,Jiří Svozilík,Tayyab Naseer,Faisal Javed,M. Zeeshan Gul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究极端黑洞霍金温度趋于零是否意味着信息擦除无需能量转移。在正则Bardeen几何中，以两级量子系统（量子记忆）耦合外部腔中受限标量场，给出擦除下界 \(Q_\infty\ge\Delta_{R,\infty}(1/2-\varepsilon)\)，并表明共振单模协议可在无外部做功下达到该界，即记忆自身供能；因此对该重置操作而言，霍金温度消失并不迫使转移热量消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landauer原理将信息擦除的最小能量代价与温度相联系；极端黑洞霍金温度趋零，热Landauer尺度亦趋零，由此产生一个自然疑问：是否可以在无能量转移的情况下擦除信息？作者希望在正则（非奇异）Bardeen黑洞背景下，用可控的量子记忆—场模型定量检验这一直觉。

Result: 得到下界 \(Q_\infty\ge\Delta_{R,\infty}(1/2-\varepsilon)\)（\(\Delta_{R,\infty}\) 为储层能隙，\(\varepsilon\) 为重置误差）。共振单模协议在外部控制不输入功的情况下达到该界，能量由记忆本身提供。多模计算（保留反向旋转项）显示，在脉冲参数不变时越接近极端，残差重置误差越大。热储层情形下，Reeb–Wolf平衡中的相对熵贡献可具有有限的低温极限；这些数值制备为外部施加，其温度仍高于霍金温度。无约束场给出 \(P_\infty\propto y^4\)，准静态假设下 \(y\propto t^{-1/2}\)，但不足以确立稳定遗迹的存在。

Conclusion: 腔中场的有限能隙使得即便霍金温度为零，擦除仍伴随非零的最小热/能量代价；对这一特定重置操作，极端黑洞霍金温度的消失并不强制转移热量为零。同时，近极端光度衰减与 \(y\propto t^{-1/2}\) 的准静态推演并不能支撑稳定遗迹的结论。

Abstract: Does the vanishing Hawking temperature of an extremal black hole imply that information can be erased without energy transfer? The Hawking temperature tends to zero at extremality, as does the corresponding thermal Landauer scale. The field in an exterior cavity behaves differently, provided both walls remain away from the horizon, its lowest excitation energy stays finite. For a maximally mixed memory coupled to a reservoir initially in its unique ground state, we find $Q_\infty\geqΔ_{R,\infty}(1/2-ε)$, with $Δ_{R,\infty}$ the reservoir gap and $ε$ the reset error. For a resonant single-mode interaction, we give a reset protocol that attains the bound with no work supplied by the external control. In this example, the memory itself supplies the energy gained by the field. In the multimode calculation, counter-rotating terms are retained. Keeping the pulse parameters unchanged, we find a larger residual reset error closer to extremality. For reservoirs prepared thermally, we evaluate the Reeb--Wolf balance and find that the relative-entropy contribution, expressed in energy units, can have a finite low-temperature limit. These numerical preparations are externally imposed and remain hotter than the Hawking temperature. For comparison, an unconfined scalar field has near-extremal luminosity $P_\infty\propto y^4$, with $y=r_H/|g|-\sqrt2$. Assuming a particular quasistatic energy-loss prescription gives $y\propto t^{-1/2}$; this does not establish the existence of a stable remnant. For this particular reset operation, vanishing Hawking temperature therefore does not force the transferred heat to vanish.

We further separate fixed-$\ell$ from fixed-$Λ_e$ parameter protocols and audit the optical effective-mass degeneracy using Lorentzian curvature invariants, timelike geodesics, and a massive Klein--Gordon probe. Throughout, the gravitational completion, prescribed fields, and continuum scattering results are interpreted only within the scope of their respective constructions. Device-level graphene transport and a self-consistent gravitational wormhole require additional physical input.

Abstract: We present an alternative formulation of coupled gravitational and electromagnetic perturbations of Kerr--Newman--de Sitter black holes, yielding decoupled radial and angular equations with a well-defined $Λ\rightarrow 0$ limit. All of these modes are consistent with the linear stability of the Kerr--Newman--de Sitter black hole. While increasing the rotation of the black hole generally favours the strong cosmic censorship conjecture, increasing the electric charge can promote its violation. We also find that the region of parameter space exhibiting a violation of the conjecture is comparatively smaller for gravitational perturbations than for electromagnetic perturbations. These results highlight the important role of rotation, electric charge, and the nature of perturbations in determining the validity of the strong cosmic censorship conjecture in Kerr--Newman--de Sitter spacetimes.

*David S. Pereira,Udaykrishna Thattarampilly,Vishnu Kakkat*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Gravity from Entropy (GfE)框架下考察纯标量部门的静态球对称黑洞，利用精确积分恒等式证明：当采样标量与径向辅助场本征值为正、且有效质量平方 m²+ξR 非负时，无穷远处趋于零的正则标量必须为零，从而排除 m²≥0 的最小耦合静态标量毛发；并借助微扰真空几何的曲率定位非最小耦合可规避符号论证的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要判定GfE中静态球对称黑洞是否允许正则标量毛发（即无标量毛定理），并厘清最小耦合与非最小耦合情形的差异，明确非最小耦合能否以及何时突破符号论证。

Method: 从完整的辅助场方程出发，将Dirac–Kähler物质方程分解为一个标量方程与一个两形式相容性条件（径向标量自动满足后者）；推导适用于一般有反作用静态球对称几何的精确积分恒等式；对 m²+ξR≥0、辅助场本征值为正的正则解作符号论证；并给出微扰GfE真空几何，以其曲率确定非最小耦合可能规避该论证的区域；最后用更精细的估计检验小范围非最小耦合能否产生自真空分岔的零模。

Result: 正则且在无穷远处趋于零的标量，在采样标量与径向辅助场本征值为正、m²+ξR≥0时必为零；特别地，在正则的同一连通分支上排除 m²≥0 的最小耦合静态标量毛发。微扰真空几何的曲率给出非最小耦合可规避符号论证的区域；进一步估计表明，仅使 m²+ξR 为负并不足以产生正则静态零模——存在一段有限区间的非最小耦合值，其下不存在从真空解分岔的分支。

Conclusion: GfE纯标量部门中静态球对称正则解不存在 m²≥0 的最小耦合标量毛发；非最小耦合虽是规避符号论证的必要条件，但仅改变 m²+ξR 的符号并不充分，需要额外的定量条件才可能出现分岔的静态零模。

Abstract: We investigate static, spherically symmetric black holes in Gravity from Entropy (GfE), focusing on the sector in which the bosonic form-space field is a pure scalar. Starting from the full auxiliary-field equations, we show that the Dirac--Kähler matter equation contains both a scalar equation and a two-form consistency condition. The latter is automatically satisfied by a radial scalar. We derive an exact integral identity, valid for a general backreacted static spherical geometry, and prove that a regular scalar approaching zero at infinity must vanish if the sampled scalar and radial auxiliary-field eigenvalues are positive and the effective mass squared $m^2+ξR$ is nonnegative. In particular, this excludes minimally coupled static scalar hair with $m^2\geq0$ on the regular identity-connected branch. We also present the perturbative GfE vacuum geometry and use its curvature to identify the region in which nonminimal coupling can evade the sign argument. A second, sharper estimate shows that making $m^2+ξR$ negative is not by itself sufficient to produce a regular static zero mode: a finite interval of nonminimal couplings still admits no branch bifurcating from the vacuum solution.

</details>


### [78] [Generalized Schwarzian thermodynamics from gravitational perturbations of near-extremal Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09757)
*Marcela Cárdenas,Benjamín Cuevas-Tapia,Jessica Trespalacios*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 直接从四维近极端Kerr近视界几何的引力微扰出发，导出广义Schwarzian热力学：两类精确线性微扰均满足JT方程且无锥奇点，其渐近对称生成时间重参数化代数，规范参数依赖Schwarzian模时等距条件选出稳态KdV层级；Euclidean区Hamiltonian作用给出奇数幂熵修正塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端Kerr的Schwarzian热力学此前多由低维或对称性约化论证得到，缺乏从四维引力微扰的严格推导；本文旨在建立其与四维近视界几何扰动、JT对偶及渐近对称性的直接联系。

Method: 在near-NHEK背景上构造两族精确线性微扰：非归一化模（改变渐近边界）和归一化模（支撑在视界附近）。二者均为真空Einstein方程约化为对偶标量JT方程的平滑解，无锥奇点。分析其渐近对称性，得到时间重参数化代数；令规范参数依赖Schwarzian模，由等距条件选出稳态KdV层级，并验证同一层级由微扰系数的真空Einstein方程独立给出。最后在Euclidean区计算Hamiltonian作用。

Result: 两类微扰模各自独立地生成近极端熵的奇数幂修正塔；KdV层级由等距条件和微扰系数的Einstein方程两条独立途径一致得到。

Conclusion: 广义Schwarzian热力学可由四维近视界引力微扰严格导出，其代数结构与KdV层级为内禀性质，且各模独立贡献熵的高阶奇数幂修正。

Abstract: We derive the generalized Schwarzian thermodynamics of near-extremal Kerr directly from four-dimensional gravitational perturbations of the near-horizon geometry. We construct two families of exact linear perturbations of the near-NHEK background: a non-normalizable mode that deforms the asymptotic boundary, and a normalizable mode supported near the horizon. Both perturbations are smooth solutions of the vacuum Einstein equations reduced to the Jackiw--Teitelboim equation for the dual scalar, without conical singularities. We study their asymptotic symmetries and show that they generate an algebra of time reparametrizations; requiring the gauge parameters to depend on the Schwarzian mode, the isometry condition selects the stationary KdV hierarchy. The same hierarchy is independently enforced by the vacuum Einstein equations on the perturbation coefficient. In the Euclidean regime, we evaluate the Hamiltonian action and show that each mode independently generates a tower of odd-power corrections to the near-extremal entropy.

</details>


### [79] [Particle production from noncommutative-inspired Reissner-Nordström black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09859)
*Alberto Maya,Marco Maceda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用无质量Klein-Gordon场与运动镜模型，计算了由涂抹（smeared）质量与电荷分布驱动的非对易启发式Reissner-Nordström黑洞的粒子产生谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Reissner-Nordström黑洞的辐射谱与量子引力修正下（非对易几何）的预期可能不同，需要考察非对易效应对霍金辐射谱的影响，特别是对外部表面引力的修正如何改变发射谱。

Method: 采用无质量Klein-Gordon场框架与运动镜（moving mirror）模型，构造非对易启发式黑洞对应的截断轨迹（truncated trajectories），并据此计算粒子产生谱。

Result: 非对易截断轨迹在高频区域显著压低发射谱，其根源在于非对易效应对黑洞外部表面引力的修正；这一结果与经典Reissner-Nordström黑洞的谱形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 非对易几何对黑洞外部表面引力的修正会明显改变高频率辐射谱，使其偏离经典RN黑洞行为，表明非对易效应在黑洞辐射中可观测/可区分。

Abstract: By considering a massless Klein-Gordon field and the moving mirror model, we calculate particle production for a noncommutative-inspired Reissner-Nordström black hole sourced by smeared distributions of mass and charge. We show that noncommutative truncated trajectories diminish the emission spectrum at large frequencies, due to noncommutative effects on the external surface gravity of the black hole, in sharp contrast with the spectrum of a classical Reissner-Nordström black hole.

</details>

### [80] [Enforcing Symmetries on Pre-trained ML Models for Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08992)
*Wenjie Zhou,Valentino F. Foit,David W. Hogg,Soledad Villar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了事后群平均（即测试时增强）在非紧致群对称性下的推广：紧致群情形可借助归一化Haar测度定义Reynolds算子，非紧致群则提出Weyl酉技巧、数据依赖正交投影与框架平均三种替代方案，并在单位缩放群与Lorentz群上做理论与实验分析，结论是事后对称化可推广至非紧致群，但不如直接训练对称模型稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后群平均无需改变架构或重新训练即可给预测器施加对称性，但已有理论保证依赖归一化Haar测度，仅适用于有限或紧致群；对非紧致群（如单位缩放群、Lorentz群）该测度不可归一化，因此需要研究替代的对称化构造及其实际有效性。

Method: 首先在物理仿真模型上做数值实验，验证紧致群下归一化Haar测度平均（Reynolds算子）的有效性。随后处理非紧致群，提出三种替代方案：(1) Weyl酉技巧——复化空间并扩展预测器；(2) 数据依赖正交投影——在不变/等变函数空间上求解非平凡优化问题；(3) 框架平均——将每点映射到一组轨道代表元。在单位缩放群与Lorentz群上进行理论刻画与实证评估。

Result: 非紧致情形下，Weyl技巧与框架平均相对基线并无一致的性能提升，仅在测试数据分布外（OOD）时出现数量级改善，Weyl技巧在低数据情形也有收益；正交投影在分布内与分布外均可带来改善；此外，直接在不变特征上训练的模型达到或超过对无约束模型做投影的效果。

Conclusion: 事后对称化可以扩展到非紧致群，但其效果不能稳定超过直接训练对称模型；实践中若追求稳健收益，应优先考虑在不变特征上训练等变/不变模型，而非依赖事后对称化。

Abstract: Post-hoc group averaging (or test-time augmentation) is a simple way to impose symmetry on a trained predictor without changing its architecture or retraining it. For finite and compact groups, averaging over a normalized Haar measure on the group gives the Reynolds operator: a canonical projection onto invariant or equivariant predictors with known non-increasing risk guarantees under certain assumptions. This paper provides numerical experiments on physics emulation models that demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in this setting. We then extend this framework to non-compact group symmetries. For non-compact groups, the Haar measure cannot be normalized, so group averaging cannot be defined in the same way. We replace it with three alternatives: the Weyl unitary trick (which requires complexifying the space and extending the predictor), a data-dependent orthogonal projection (which involves a non-trivial optimization on the space of invariant or equivariant functions), and a frame averaging approach (which involves mapping each point to a set of orbit representatives). We illustrate the constructions theoretically and empirically on the unit-rescaling group and the Lorentz group. Our results show that in the non-compact case, the Weyl trick and frame averaging do not provide a consistent improvement over the baseline, except where the test data is out-of-distribution (an orders-of-magnitude improvement in this case) and, for the Weyl trick, in low-data regimes. The orthogonal projection can provide improvements in-distribution and out-of-distribution. We also observe that models trained directly on invariant features matched or beat the projection of an unconstrained one. We conclude that post-hoc symmetrization extends to non-compact groups, but it does not consistently outperform training a symmetric model.

</details>


### [81] [FasterRW: An Efficient Prior-Assisted Random Walk Solver for Thermal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08490)
*Zixiao Wang,Tianshu Hou,Chenghan Wang,Zhen Zhuang,Leilei Jin,Tsung-Yi Ho,Farzan Farnia,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 FasterRW，一种基于“延续值（continuation value）”框架的 Feynman–Kac 热学随机游走加速方法，通过停止时刻残差恒等式与先验校正的单点估计器，以及将跨查询穿出尾部转为伪样本并用 kriging 在“先验误差坐标”中精化的多点查询机制，在保持精度的前提下最高实现 28× 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热仿真在现代 IC 设计与制造中日益关键。Feynman–Kac 随机游走方法无需解全局场即可估计局部温度，但在实际缺乏 Dirichlet 边界的场景中，为控制截断残差需要极长的路径，导致效率低下。

Method: 1) 证明停止时刻残差恒等式：截断后的期望尾部等于当前衰减权重乘以停止位置的温度；2) 据此构造先验校正的单点估计器，用廉价含噪先验替代未知延续值，使截断偏差随先验误差而非绝对温度缩放，并给出误差可控的阈值选择规则；3) 多点查询时，将跨查询的穿出（pass-through）尾部转化为伪样本，并用 kriging 在先验误差坐标中精化观测。

Result: 与先前最先进方法相比，在保持精度的同时最高获得 28× 加速；代码已开源于 https://github.com/ShiningSord/FastRW。

Conclusion: 通过延续值与先验校正视角重构 Feynman–Kac 随机游走的截断问题，FasterRW 在无 Dirichlet 边界的实际热仿真场景中显著缓解长路径需求，兼顾精度与效率，为 IC 热分析提供了实用的加速框架。

Abstract: Thermal simulation is becoming increasingly critical in modern IC design and manufacturing. Feynman--Kac random walk methods can estimate localized temperatures without solving the global field, but in practical settings without Dirichlet boundaries they require very long paths to control the truncation residual. This paper presents FasterRW, a continuation-value framework for accelerating Feynman--Kac thermal random walks. We prove a stopping-time residual identity showing that the expected tail after truncation equals the current attenuation weight multiplied by the temperature at the stopping location. This identity enables a prior-corrected single-point estimator: a cheap, noisy prior replaces the unknown continuation value, making the truncation bias scale with prior error rather than absolute temperature and yielding an error-controlled threshold-selection rule. For multi-point queries, FasterRW further converts cross-query pass-through tails into pseudo-samples and refines the resulting observations in prior-error coordinates through kriging. Compared with prior state-of-the-art methods, FasterRW achieves up to $28\times$ acceleration while maintaining accuracy. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/ShiningSord/FastRW.

</details>


### [82] [Embedding Consistent Recurrent Neural Networks in Finite Element Simulations for Path-Dependent Damage Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08494)
*Muhammed Adil Yatkın,Mihkel Kõrgesaar,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将训练好的RNN损伤准则用Fortran重写并嵌入Abaqus/Explicit的VUMAT中作为逐增量断裂准则，提出状态转移函数经设计以保证应变路径细化时收敛的ConsRNN，并与SimpleRNN对比；结果表明离散一致性是嵌入循环代理模型的决定性属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性显式有限元中，应变增量步数难以控制，要求代理模型在不同增量步数下给出与求解器一致、稳定的预测；而普通RNN的预测会随增量步数漂移，无法直接嵌入。

Method: 用Fortran重新实现已训练的循环神经网络损伤准则，作为VUMAT中的在线断裂准则，使网络状态随求解器同步推进；对比SimpleRNN与所提出的ConsRNN（其转移函数设计使得随应变路径细化预测收敛而非随增量数漂移）；两者均以双线性应变路径训练，并在不同时间离散化和非线性加载历史下评估；随后在结构尺度上嵌入ConsRNN控制夹持钢板单元删除，与既有两参数断裂准则比较。

Result: SimpleRNN的预测随增量数增加而漂移，不适合嵌入；ConsRNN在细化下收敛，但在多线性路径上相对SimpleRNN在固定分辨率下有明显精度损失。结构尺度上，嵌入的ConsRNN与两参数断裂准则得到相同的峰值力和峰值位移（峰值前无单元删除、本构路径相同，故峰值前一致属构造使然），差异仅出现在峰后软化段。

Conclusion: 离散化一致性是循环代理模型嵌入显式有限元求解器的决定性属性；工作首次在结构尺度实现了具有非标准转移函数的定制循环单元的嵌入。

Abstract: Capturing sheet-metal localisation physics with a trained surrogate inside a finite element (FEM) solver requires the surrogate to deliver consistent predictions irrespective of the solver's strain-increment count, which is difficult to control in non-linear explicit codes. We address this by re-implementing a trained recurrent neural network (RNN) damage criterion in Fortran and embed it as a live, increment-by-increment fracture criterion inside an Abaqus/Explicit user material subroutine (VUMAT), advancing the network state alongside the solver. Two architectures are compared: a SimpleRNN and the proposed Consistent RNN (ConsRNN), whose transition function is designed so that predictions converge as the strain path is refined rather than drifting with the increment count. Both are trained on bilinear strain paths and evaluated under varying temporal discretisations and nonlinear histories. SimpleRNN predictions drift as the increment count increases, which disqualifies it for embedding; ConsRNN converges under refinement, at a substantial fixed-resolution accuracy cost relative to SimpleRNN on multilinear paths. Deployed at structural scale, the embedded ConsRNN surrogate governs element deletion in a clamped steel plate and reaches the same peak force and peak displacement as an established two-parameter fracture criterion. This pre-peak agreement is expected by construction, since no element is deleted before the peak and the two runs therefore share an identical constitutive path; the responses diverge only on the post-peak softening branch. The results establish discretisation consistency as the decisive property for embedding recurrent surrogates in explicit FEM solvers, and report the first embedding of a customised, non-standard-transition recurrent cell at structural scale.

</details>


### [83] [Rethinking Data Selection as Distribution Matching on Semantic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08495)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Mingshuo Chen,Chunxiao Li,Yao Zhu,Huawen Shen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QMAC，一种质量感知的多频带特征函数匹配数据选择方法，将数据选择建模为分布匹配与质量过滤的联合优化问题，在4类数据、8个模型、44个基准上优于现有基线并多次超过全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调依赖大规模训练数据，带来巨大计算与存储开销，且数据中存在冗余、噪声与低质量样本。现有数据选择方法多基于多样性、重要性或二者结合，未显式约束所选子集与全量数据在嵌入空间中的分布一致性。

Method: 将数据选择表述为分布匹配与质量过滤的联合优化问题：在多个频率尺度上匹配所选子集与全量数据的经验特征函数（characteristic function），从而同时捕捉全局语义覆盖与局部扰动；并结合任务相关的质量分数过滤噪声与低质量样本。

Result: 在4种数据类型、8个目标模型、44个基准上系统评估：在标准图文指令数据上优于现有数据选择基线；在标准文本指令、图文推理与文本推理数据上显著超过随机选择；在多种模型与预算组合下超过全量数据训练效果。

Conclusion: 显式约束嵌入空间中的分布一致性并联合质量过滤，可有效提升指令微调数据选择的效率与效果，实现少数据优于全量训练；代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Instruction tuning of large language models and large vision language models relies on massive training data, which incurs substantial computational and storage costs. Meanwhile, training data often contains redundancy, noise, and low quality samples. Existing data selection methods mostly rely on diversity, importance, or a combination of both, without explicitly constraining the distributional consistency between the selected subset and the full data in the embedding space. We propose QMAC, a quality aware multiband characteristic function matching method that formulates data selection as a joint optimization problem of distribution matching and quality filtering. QMAC matches the empirical characteristic functions of the selected subset and the full data across multiple frequency scales, capturing both global semantic coverage and local perturbations, while using task related quality scores to filter noisy and low quality samples. We systematically evaluate QMAC on 4 data types, 8 target models, and 44 benchmarks. Experimental results show that QMAC outperforms existing data selection baselines on standard image-text instruction data, substantially surpasses random selection on standard text instruction, image-text reasoning, and text reasoning data, and exceeds full data training in multiple model and budget combinations. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [84] [Generative Reconstruction of Full-Field Sensor States for Safety-Critical Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08499)
*Umme Mahbuba Nabila,Linyu Lin,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究为OSU高温试验装置(HTTF)构建稀疏传感器重构框架，在实验内与跨实验两种协议下比较六种方法，结果表明GNN-GRU-VAE在实验内误差最低(MAE 11.73°C, RMSE 20.54°C, R²=0.98)，分段上下文扩散模型跨实验迁移最佳(RMSE 42.84°C, R²=0.91)，验证了时空生成建模在安全关键数字孪生缺失传感器重构中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键数字孪生需要准确的传感器场重建以实现状态感知，但高温辐照环境中的热电偶会漂移、失效或间歇性不可用，导致部分可观测性，因此需发展稀疏传感器重构方法。

Method: 在HTTF数据上评估六类方法：三种条件生成模型(DM、VAE、GAN)、两种确定性RNN(纯GRU与图神经网络-门控循环单元GNN-GRU)以及岭回归；实验内协议在PG-29失压导热冷却(DCC)试验上采用图空间编码+GRU，跨实验协议以PG-27加压导热冷却(PCC)训练、在PG-29测试并采用分段上下文条件化。

Result: 实验内：GNN-GRU-VAE最优(MAE 11.73°C、RMSE 20.54°C、R²=0.98)；确定性GNN-GRU与纯GRU的RMSE分别为25.26°C与35.22°C，显示空间建模收益；三种生成模型RMSE均较岭回归降低一半以上；已知传感器由91降至45个时DM仍保持R²=0.94。跨实验：分段上下文DM迁移最佳(RMSE 42.84°C、R²=0.91)。

Conclusion: 时空生成建模对安全关键数字孪生中的缺失传感器重构具有显著价值，图结构空间编码与生成式模型的结合在精度、鲁棒性和跨工况迁移方面均优于确定性模型与线性基线。

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of incomplete sensor fields is critical for state awareness in safety-critical digital twins (DTs). Thermocouples in high-temperature and radiation environments may drift, fail, or become intermittently unavailable, causing partial observability. This study develops a sparse-sensor reconstruction framework for Oregon State University's High Temperature Test Facility (HTTF) and evaluates six methods under intra- and inter-experiment protocols: three conditional generative models (i.e., a diffusion model [DM], variational autoencoder [VAE], and generative adversarial network [GAN]), two deterministic recurrent neural-network models, and ridge regression.
